### scripts/_verify_orphan.js
- Line 28: regex `aRe` has a negative lookahead `(?![\w$])` after `__CX_FORCE_PLAY`. It also allows optional `(?:window\.)?`. The regex: `\b(?:var|let|const)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\s*=\s*\(?\s*(?:window\.)?__CX_FORCE_PLAY(?![\w$])\s*(?=[;,)\n])`. 
- Problem: The `\b` before `(?:var|let|const)` requires a word boundary, but after a newline or semicolon it's fine. However, the regex `(?=[;,)\n])` at end means it matches only if followed by `;`, `,`, `)`, or newline. But what about `const ns = __CX_FORCE_PLAY; // comment`? After `__CX_FORCE_PLAY` is space then `;` - the lookahead matches `;`. Good.
- What about `const ns = (__CX_FORCE_PLAY);`? The regex has `\(?` optional opening paren, then `(?:window\.)?__CX_FORCE_PLAY`, then `(?![\w$])`, then `(?=[;,)\n])`. For `(__CX_FORCE_PLAY)`, after `__CX_FORCE_PLAY` is `)`, so lookahead matches. Good.
- Issue: it doesn't capture `toolkit` alias? Actually root is toolkit which is separate. It captures `__CX_FORCE_PLAY` aliases.
- Potential issue: `roots` at line 33 includes `__CX_FORCE_PLAY` and aliases but not `toolkit`. But the script title says "命名空间符号" and `toolkit` is part of it? Actually toolkit is mounted on __CX_FORCE_PLAY.toolkit, so reading toolkit.X is reading __CX_FORCE_PLAY.toolkit.X. The regex for reads uses `__CX_FORCE_PLAY` roots only, so `toolkit.X` reads won't be counted. But the script comment says it focuses on `__CX_FORCE_PLAY.X`. This is a limitation but not necessarily a bug given its scope.
- Potential issue: line 37 `const ln = t.slice(0, m.index).split('\n').length;` computes line number but doesn't use it for reads. Only for mounts it uses ln. Not a bug, just unused.
- Potential issue: line 45 line number for mounts, fine.

Let me also look at some specific patterns. The `h()` helper has a potential issue: at line 339 `if (('on' + _evt) in el) el.addEventListener(_evt, v); else el.setAttribute(k, v);`. Setting a function as attribute value via `setAttribute(k, v)` when v is a function will call toString() on the function. That's not an XSS issue per se, but it's a logic issue: if the attribute name doesn't correspond to a real event, it sets the function string as attribute. But this is by design.

Actually, more importantly, `h()` creates elements with `document.createElement(tag)` where `tag` comes from caller. If tag is not a string or is malicious, it could create weird elements. But internal use only.

#### popup-quiz.js
Many issues:
1. Logic error: `_pqHandle` marks dialog as handled (`_pqHandled[fp] = true`) before actions complete (line 144). If `_pqPickRandom`, `_pqClickAnswer`, or `_pqDismiss` throws (inside bestEffort caught) or fails, the dialog won't be retried next tick, leaving it stuck. Since bestEffort catches exceptions, no throw, but if click doesn't actually dismiss (e.g., event handler not triggered), still marked handled. Potential logic error. Could mark after success.
2. Logic error: `_pqPickRandom` clicks a random option then `_pqClickAnswer` clicks submit; but if option click opens a sub-dialog or requires wait, immediate submit may fail. Not a clear bug.
3. Potential runtime exception: `_pqIsDialogCandidate` calls `el.getBoundingClientRect()` without checking if element is connected to DOM or if it's in a shadow root? `getBoundingClientRect` works on disconnected? returns zeros. Fine.
4. Performance bottleneck: `_pqQueryAll` (lines 62-75) iterates over broad dialogSels including `[class*="popup"]` and pushes duplicates, then `.filter(_pqIsDialogCandidate)` calls `querySelectorAll` again for each candidate with optionSels and button selectors. On large pages, each tick can be expensive.
5. Performance bottleneck: The fallback loop in popupQuizTick (lines 211-218) scans all `button, a[class*="btn"]` in document each tick, performing text regex and `closest()`. Expensive on large DOM and repeated every tick.
6. Logic/Security: `_pqDismiss` and `_pqRemove` remove elements from DOM. If `_pqIsDialogCandidate` misclassifies an important container (e.g., because it contains a button with text "确定" or class 'modal'), the script can delete page content. The candidate filter has risks. This is a runtime/security issue.

If tick runs frequently and each call increments count even with handled=0, storm gate may silence after 8 calls in 10s regardless. Actually line 30 increments before checking; any call increments. So if no dialogs but tick runs every second, after 8 ticks it will silence for 30s. This is a logic/performance issue: it counts attempts, not actual dismissals. The comment says "10秒内关闭超过 8 个弹窗" but implementation counts invocations. Potential false storm. Could move increment after actual handle/block.

If CONST undefined, ReferenceError caught by bestEffort, prune silently fails. But then _pqHandled grows unbounded over long sessions. Potential memory leak/runtime exception. However maybe CONST defined.

#### zhihuishu-exam.js
Detailed issues:
1. Logic/timing: IIFE site guard at line 20 executes at script parse. If `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY` is not yet initialized (e.g., if this content script runs before core), the module exits early and never re-runs, even after core loads. Actually content script files run once per injection; if it exits, it won't run again. So if core loads later, module is dead. Potential runtime/logic issue. Similar for other addons, but here it's more critical. Suggest use polling/wait or rely on core to load this module after initialization.

For `<label>` options wrapping checkboxes/radios, clicking the label already toggles the input; setting checked=true and clicking again may toggle it off or cause double event dispatch. This can result in option not being selected or repeated attempts. Logic error.

However `_isExamPage` regex may match pages that aren't actual exam (e.g., '/homeworking' fails due lookahead, but '/homework-assignment' after slash: 'homework' followed by '-'? '/homeworks' after 'homework' is 's' -> fails. Good.

However onRoute calls reSync inside bestEffort? Actually reSync is `sm.sync` not wrapped. `sync()` uses bestEffort internally? It doesn't wrap everything; some calls inside safeGet. If sm.sync throws (e.g., localStorage), history override propagates exception to caller. This can break site navigation. Potential runtime exception. Should wrap onRoute in try/catch/bestEffort.

If synchronous, script execution may be aborted. Minor.

But they passed, so either site-router handles renwei.com or the tick function doesn't check site strictly. Not a clear bug, but the URL inconsistency is suspicious. I'll report it as a potential issue.

16. `scripts/verify_contracts.js:83` and `scripts/verify_contract_doc.js:71` - mountRe with `(?!=)(?!=)` double negative lookahead may misclassify `=>` or `+=`? Let me re-examine. Actually for `root.X += 1`, the regex matches `root.X +=` because after `root.X` there's `\s*=` matching ` =`, then `(?!=)` checks next char is `+` (not =) passes, `(?!=)` checks next char is `1` (not =) passes. So it would treat `+=` as a mount. But this is unlikely to occur for namespace properties. For `root.X => expr`, after `root.X` is ` =>`, `\s*=` matches ` =`, `(?!=)` next is `>` passes, next is space passes. So it would treat arrow function as mount. But again, arrow function for namespace property is unusual. This is a minor false positive possibility. I'll mention it briefly.

17. `_sim/verify_fixes.js:9` - brittle check `'ninja_toast_fix': s.includes('n 模式已') && (s.indexOf('开') < s.indexOf('关') || ...)`. This is a maintainability issue.

18. `scripts/verify_contract_semantics.js:185` - regex `/delete\s+\w+\.pause\s*;\s*delete\s+\w+\.play/` assumes exact order and syntax. If source uses bracket notation or different variable names, it fails. This is a maintainability/brittleness issue.

19. `_sim/sim-iframe.js` - The iframe path always skips in jsdom. It documents this and mentions Playwright coverage. This is acceptable but I can note it as a coverage gap.

Now, let me think about the issue with `window.top.document` in panel-core.js more carefully. The code at line 39:

```js
var pd = (window.top && window.top.document && window.top.document.body) ? window.top.document : document;
```

In a cross-origin iframe, `window.top` is the top window object but accessing `.document` throws. So `window.top && window.top.document` will throw `SecurityError`. This is a runtime exception.

However, there's a subtlety: in some environments, reading `window.top.document` from a cross-origin iframe does throw. So this is a valid issue.

The fix would be to wrap in try/catch.

Similarly, in `panel-toast-feed.js`, `_findFeedBox` does:
```js
var b = (typeof document !== 'undefined') ? document.getElementById('__cxToastFeed') : null;
if (!b && window.top && window.top.document) b = window.top.document.getElementById('__cxToastFeed');
```
This is inside safeGet, so cross-origin exception is caught and returns null. Good.

In `diagnostics.js` `_cxAuditDomPresent`:
```js
bestEffort(function () {
  if (document.getElementById(id)) { _present = true; return; }
  if (window.top && window.top !== window && window.top.document && window.top.document.getElementById(id)) { _present = true; return; }
}, 'diag.auditDom');
```
Inside bestEffort, so caught. Good.

So panel-core.js line 39 is the one not wrapped.

# 代码审查报告

## 1. commands.js

Issue 1: innerHTML 动态生成命令下拉
- Line 154, 172
- Security + maintainability
- Code: `box.innerHTML = '';` and `box.innerHTML = html;`
- Consequence: 虽然使用 escapeHTML 转义了命令名/描述，但仍使用 innerHTML 注入动态内容。若 escapeHTML 被绕过或未来维护者忘记转义，会导致 XSS。同时与项目倡导的 h()/setSafeText DOM 构建方式不一致。
- Recommendation: 使用 h() 与 setSafeText/appendChild 构建下拉项，彻底移除 innerHTML。

Issue 2: 命令下拉没有事件委托，每次重渲染都重建 DOM
- Actually it does use event delegation in panel-core.js. So not an issue.

Issue 3: `/ninja` 命令参数解析不严格
- Line 127
- Logic error
- Code: `var on = !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim());` only handles "off", not "0" or "on".
- Consequence: `/ninja 0` will turn ninja on (because "0" is not "off"), inconsistent with `/loop 0` and `/debug 0` which turn off. Also any garbage value turns it on.
- Recommendation: Parse arg explicitly: 'on'/'1' -> on, 'off'/'0' -> off, else toggle.

Issue 4: `/mute` and `/single` similar loose arg parsing
- Lines 114, 132
- Logic error
- Same pattern.
- Recommendation: unify arg parsing helper.

Issue 5: `_cxCmdOnKey` Enter logic
- Line 211
- Logic/maintainability
- Code compares `('/' + _cxCmdFilter[_cxCmdHi].name) === ('' + inp.value).trim()` then for args commands sets value with trailing space and executes. This means for args commands, after Enter it executes with empty arg (since trailing space after name means arg is empty). The commands handle empty arg, but UX may be confusing.
- Actually this is intended: pick command and wait for user to type arg. But then it executes immediately with empty arg. For /rate, empty arg -> toast "用法: /rate 0.5~2". So it's fine but could be cleaner.
- Maybe not report.

Issue 6: Favorites loaded without schema validation
- Line 12
- Potential runtime exception / logic error
- Code: `_cxFavorites = safeGet(function () { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CX_KEYS.CMD_FAV) || '[]'); }, []);`
- Consequence: If localStorage contains non-array (e.g. user tampered), `_cxFavorites.indexOf` may throw or behave unexpectedly.
- Recommendation: Validate that parsed value is array of strings.

## 2. dashboard.js

Issue 1: innerHTML in video list
- Lines 316-318
- Security + performance
- Code: `wrap.innerHTML = html;`
- Consequence: Full rebuild of video list DOM on each refresh; destroys nodes, loses non-delegated state, heavy for many videos. Security risk if escapeHTML misses.
- Recommendation: Use h()/DOM diff or at least documentFragment with setSafeText.

Issue 2: innerHTML in hero time
- Lines 44, 73
- Security/maintainability
- Code: `heroTime.innerHTML = '0:00<small> / 0:00</small>';` and `heroTime.innerHTML = tCur + '<small> / ' + tDur + '</small>';`
- Consequence: tCur/tDur are from fmtTime (numbers), so safe now, but pattern violates DOM-safe guideline.
- Recommendation: Build small element with h() and replace children.

Issue 3: `renderDashboard` hit rate sampling increments on every render
- Lines 241-243
- Logic/performance
- Code: `if (targetEnabled) { _targetHitSamples++; if (TARGET.matchedAny) _targetHitHits++; }`
- Consequence: Sample count grows with panel refresh frequency, not with actual targeting attempts. The percentage is accurate asymptotically but not representative per unit time.
- Recommendation: Sample per tick or per actual targeting decision, not per render.

Issue 4: `updateBadge` uses `v[FLAGS.endedLock]` etc. without checking FLAGS exists
- Lines 172-174
- Potential runtime exception
- Code: `if (v[FLAGS.endedLock]) hasEndLock = true;`
- Consequence: If FLAGS undefined, throws ReferenceError. However FLAGS likely defined globally.
- Recommendation: Add typeof guard or default.

Issue 5: `_refreshQuizZone` operator precedence unclear
- Line 122
- Readability
- Code: `if (!st || !st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0)`
- Consequence: Works but hard to read; risk of future edit introducing precedence bug.
- Recommendation: Add parentheses: `if (!st || (!st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0))`.

Issue 6: `renderVideoList` string diff
- Lines 316-318
- Performance
- Code compares full HTML string.
- Consequence: String comparison of large HTML is O(n) and still runs innerHTML if changed.
- Recommendation: Diff individual items by video identity.

## 3. diagnostics.js

Issue 1: fallbackCopy reports success when body absent
- Lines 140-145
- Logic error
- Code:
```js
if (document.body) { ... execCommand('copy'); ... }
EventBus.emit('ui:toast', '已复制诊断信息');
```
- Consequence: If document.body is null, still emits "已复制" even though nothing was copied.
- Recommendation: Move toast inside body branch or emit failure message in else.

Issue 2: `_loopLastTick` arithmetic with undefined can produce NaN
- Line 64
- Logic/readability
- Code: `(_loopLastTick ? (' · 上次tick=' + Math.round((Date.now() - _loopLastTick) / 1000) + 's前') : ' · 上次tick=未运行')`
- Actually it checks `_loopLastTick`. OK.
- Wait line 64: `'主循环: ' + (_loopTimer ? '运行中' : '已停') + ... + (_loopLastTick ? ... : ...)`. OK.

Issue 3: `buildInvasionReport` uses `safeGet(function () { return typeof window[gi]; }, 'undefined')` 
- Line 196
- Logic issue
- Code: `if (safeGet(function () { return typeof window[gi]; }, 'undefined') !== 'undefined') g.push(gi);`
- `typeof window[gi]` returns string. If window[gi] doesn't exist, returns 'undefined'. If exists, returns 'object' etc. So condition checks if global exists. OK. But `safeGet` fallback 'undefined' is string. OK.

Issue 4: `copyDiagnostics` catches build error but doesn't include error details
- Lines 131-133
- Maintainability
- Code: `if (txt === undefined) txt = '诊断生成失败：build 过程抛出异常（详见控制台 telemetry 日志）';`
- Consequence: User gets generic message.
- Recommendation: Include recent error tag/msg.

Issue 5: `buildDiagnostics` line 91 uses `_playWatchDocs` without guard
- Potential runtime exception if _playWatchDocs undefined. But it's defined somewhere. Not critical.

## 4. panel-controls.js

Issue 1: blackbox export document.write with partial escaping
- Lines 228-236
- Security + maintainability
- Code: `w.document.write('<pre>' + text.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</pre>');`
- Consequence: document.write is deprecated; partial escaping is fragile; could break out with `</pre>` if `<` escaping somehow bypassed.
- Recommendation: Use DOM API: w.document.body.appendChild(w.document.createElement('pre')).textContent = text.

Issue 2: review export JSON.parse without guard inside nested bestEffort
- Lines 199-209
- Logic/runtime

### 2. 逻辑错误（门禁无法真正失败）

- **文件路径与行号**: `_sim/sim-iframe.js:121`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 脚本末尾无论 `conclusion` 是 `PASS`、`FAIL` 还是 `SKIP`，都执行 `process.exit(0)`。
  ```js
  console.log('\n结论: ' + conclusion);
  window.close();
  process.exit(0);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 即使顶层产物加载抛错或按 P 键路径异常，CI 也会判定为通过，失去回归意义。
- **改进建议**: 根据实际失败条件返回非零退出码，例如 `process.exit(conclusion === 'PASS' ? 0 : 1)`。

---

- **文件路径与行号**: `_sim/sim-ninja.js:56`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 结论计算为 `CHECK` 时仍调用 `process.exit(0)`。
  ```js
  console.log('\n结论: ' + (p && ninjaAgain && ... ? 'PASS — ...' : 'CHECK'));
  process.exit(0);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: Ninja 模式断言失败时不会导致构建门禁失败。
- **改进建议**: 维护 `fail` 计数器，并在最后根据 `fail` 返回 `0` 或 `1`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `_sim/sim-ui.js:160`

  ```js

  window.close();
  process.exit(0);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 面板命令测试或循环后产生的错误无法中断 CI。
- **改进建议**: 在 `process.exit(0)` 前判断 `loadErr || errors.length > 0`，满足则 `process.exit(1)`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `scripts/selftest_contract_semantics.js:52` 与 `_sim/sim-uninstall.js:52`

### 4. 静态分析器的实现缺陷 / 脆弱性

- **文件路径与行号**: `scripts/verify_contract_semantics.js:49` 与 `scripts/verify_contract_doc.js:81`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 通过 `indexOf('{', ...)` 定位函数体/对象字面量起始，未处理函数签名注释中可能包含 `{` 的情况，且未识别模板字符串中的 `{`。
  ```js
  var i = code.indexOf('{', m.index);
  // ...
  const i = after.indexOf('{');
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 若源码在函数名与 `{` 之间插入含 `{` 的注释，或在模板字符串 `${}` 内嵌套大括号，后续大括号配对会错位，导致断言漏报或误报。
- **改进建议**: 在 `extractFunction` 中先跳过注释，并将反引号模板字符串纳入字符串状态处理。

---

- **文件路径与行号**: `scripts/verify_contract_semantics.js:198`
- **问题分类**: 可读性/maintainability 缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 用硬编码阈值 `20` 判断 `cleanupListeners` 中 `bestEffort` 包裹数量。
  ```js
  assert(deletes >= 20, 'C4.3', ..., '还原动作的 bestEffort 包裹数异常偏少（' + deletes + '）');
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 一旦卸载函数重构导致 `bestEffort` 调用次数变化（即使语义正确），门禁会无理由失败；反之，数量达标也不代表每个还原动作都被包裹。
- **改进建议**: 改为对必须包裹的还原动作逐条断言，而非依赖魔法数字。

---

- **文件路径与行号**: `scripts/verify_contract_semantics.js:185`
- **问题分类**: 可读性/maintainability 缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 实例级 play/pause 还原断言使用固定语法正则。
  ```js
  ['实例级 play/pause 还原', /delete\s+\w+\.pause\s*;\s*delete\s+\w+\.play/, ...]
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 若源码改为 `delete _iv['pause']`、换行或变量名变化，正则失效，产生假阳性失败。
- **改进建议**: 通过 AST 或至少允许空白/换行及可选引号的宽松正则来匹配。

---

- **文件路径与行号**: `scripts/verify_contracts.js:83` 与 `scripts/verify_contract_doc.js:71`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 挂载点正则使用 `(?!=)(?!=)` 来排除 `==`/`===`，但无法排除 `+=`、`-=` 及箭头函数 `=>`。
  ```js
  const mountRe = new RegExp('(?<!\\w)(?:window\\.)?(?:' + rootAlt + ')\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\s*=(?!=)(?!=)', 'g');
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 极少数情况下会把 `toolkit.x += 1` 或 `toolkit.x = () => ...` 误判为新的挂载点，产生错误挂载符号集合。
- **改进建议**: 将赋值检测限定为 `\s*=\s*(?![=])` 并在消费处进一步排除 `=>` 上下文，或改用更完整的 token 解析。

---

- **文件路径与行号**: `_sim/verify_fixes.js:9`
- **问题分类**: 可读性/maintainability 缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: ninja toast 修复校验逻辑极为脆弱。
  ```js
  'ninja_toast_fix': s.includes('n 模式已') && (s.indexOf('开') < s.indexOf('关') || s.match(/'\u5f00'\s*:\s*'\u5173'/) !== null),
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 只要产物中任意位置的“开”出现在“关”之前即通过，与 ninja toast 的实际修复是否到位无关。
- **改进建议**: 精确匹配与 ninja toast 相关的代码片段（如附近的 label 文本或状态变量）。

---

### 5. 覆盖率 / 回归缺口

- **文件路径与行号**: `_sim/sim-iframe.js:51-66`
- **问题分类**: 可读性/maintainability 缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: jsdom 环境下 `iframe.contentDocument.body` 为 `null`，导致完整的跨 iframe 续播路径始终被跳过。
- **潜在后果**: 该仿真在 Node/jsdom CI 中无法覆盖最核心的“焦点在 iframe 内按 P 续播”场景，只能验证顶层路径。
- **改进建议**: 已在注释中提到 Playwright 版本，建议在 CI 中显式调度 `_sim/sim-iframe-pw.js`（或等效真机脚本），避免 jsdom SKIP 掩盖回归。

Now need consider security issues: The scripts manipulate DOM, localStorage, history. The user asked security issues. Potential issues:
- `video-ended-notify.js` sends page URL/title to local bridge without user consent. That's a privacy/security concern. We can mention under security for video-ended-notify.
- `auto-next.js` navigates to URLs from DOM/Bridge without validating origin. Security issue: possible open redirect / navigation to malicious URL if DOM compromised.
- `popup-quiz.js` removes DOM elements based on heuristics; could be abused by malicious page to trick removal of important elements (self-XSS?). But script runs on arbitrary allowed sites. If site is compromised, script can be used to hide security warnings? The script itself could hide warning banners if they match dialog selectors. That's a security/usability risk. Mention.
- `zhihuishu-exam.js` overrides history methods globally. Could be used by malicious page to intercept navigation? But script is injected into page; the page itself can already do this. Not a major security issue. Could mention as tampering with native APIs.
- `keyboard-shortcuts.js` listens to keyboard globally and prevents default. Could be used by malicious page to intercept keys? Not relevant.
- `tamper-guard.js` requests Notification permission. Could be abused? Not really.

#### `src/chaoxing-force-play/plugins/addons/auto-next.js`

Issue 1: doNavigate doesn't handle query-relative URLs.
- line 283: `if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url) || url.charAt(0) === '/') { ... } else if (url.charAt(0) === '#' || /javascript:/.test(url)) { ... } else { ... }`
- consequence: Bridge returns `?knowledgeId=...` or DOM link href `?...` cannot navigate, auto-next stops with LOCKED.
- recommendation: treat `url.charAt(0) === '?'` as same-document relative, use `location.href = url` or `location.search = url`.

Issue 2: findNextDOM fallback scans huge node list.
- line 235: `var clickable = $dom.all('button, [role="button"], [onclick], span, li, div');`
- consequence: performance hit on large pages; may click wrong generic "下一页".
- recommendation: scope to chapter/lesson container or use more specific selectors.

Issue 3: allDead bridge flag never resets.
- lines 101, 143, 157: `if (allDead) return Promise.reject(new Error('no bridge'));` and `allDead = true`.
- consequence: if user starts bridge later, auto-next won't recover without refresh.
- recommendation: expose reset or retry periodically.

Issue 4: handledUrls fallback keyed by src fragile.
- lines 175, 361-362: `handledUrls[v.src] = true` and `seenVideos.has(v)`.
- consequence: duplicate or empty src leads to skipped videos.
- recommendation: use WeakSet always; fallback use unique id or counter.

Issue 5: blockedByVerify false positive.
- lines 181-187, 58: BLOCK_KEYWORDS innerText check.
- consequence: normal page text containing "解锁" stops auto-next.
- recommendation: require context/element selectors.

Issue 6: DEBUG hard-coded true.
- line 30: `DEBUG: true`.
- consequence: console noise in production.
- recommendation: default false or read from config.

Issue 7: swallow global dependency in catch.
- lines 382, 386: `swallow(e);` no local fallback.
- consequence: if utils not loaded, error in catch masks original error and may throw ReferenceError.
- recommendation: define local fallback `function swallow(e){}`.

Issue 8: Navigation security.
- line 287 `window.location.href = url;` and line 274 click with urls from DOM/Bridge.
- consequence: could navigate to attacker-controlled URL if DOM compromised.
- recommendation: validate URL origin/path before navigating.

- lines 34-51, especially 48 `e.preventDefault();`.
- consequence: Space on buttons/links won't activate them; scroll disabled when focus not in text entry.
- recommendation: skip if target is interactive element (button, link, input) or use target-specific handler.

Issue 2: activeVideo doesn't check hidden styles.
- lines 17-29.
- consequence: may select hidden/invisible video.
- recommendation: check computed display/visibility.

Maybe no issue.

#### `tamper-guard.js`

Issue 2: applyVisual in quiet mode only hides existing bar, doesn't prevent new bar creation? Actually line 19-22: if quiet, get bar and hide. It returns, so doesn't create. Fine.
Maybe no other.

#### `video-ended-notify.js`

Issue 1: Doesn't create addon queue if missing.
- line 144: `if (window.__cxAddonQueue) window.__cxAddonQueue.push(addon);`
- consequence: if loaded before registry, toggle missing.
- recommendation: use `(window.__cxAddonQueue = ... || []).push(addon);`.

Issue 2: play listener missing capture.
- line 168: `document.addEventListener('play', function () {...});`
- consequence: play event doesn't bubble; restoreIfNeeded never called; videos may stay muted.
- recommendation: add `true` for capture or attach to each video.

Issue 3: Sends page title/URL to bridge.
- line 155: `b.send({ type: 'video-ended', url: location.href, title: document.title, ts: Date.now() });`
- consequence: privacy leak to local bridge.
- recommendation: document behavior and allow opt-out.

Issue 4: No cleanup for listeners.
- lines 163-168, no __cxAddonCleanups.
- consequence: listeners persist after script unload/disable.

#### `zhihuishu-auto-next.js`

Issue 1: Early site guard exits if core not ready.
- line 15: `if (!(window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY && ... detectSite() === 'zhihuishu')) return;`
- consequence: if executed before core, module never initializes even after core loads.
- recommendation: poll/wait for __CX_FORCE_PLAY or defer until core ready.

Issue 2: No cleanup for storage listener.
- line 49.
- minor.

#### `popup-quiz.js`

Issue 1: _pqHandle marks dialog handled before actions succeed.
- lines 142-153: `_pqHandled[fp] = true;` then inside bestEffort pick/click/dismiss.
- consequence: if action fails (e.g., click didn't dismiss), no retry; dialog stuck.
- recommendation: mark handled only after successful dismiss.

Issue 2: Storm counter increments per tick, not per dismissal.
- lines 25-40, line 30 `_pqStormGate.count++` before any dialog processed.
- consequence: frequent ticks cause false storm silence even with no popups.
- recommendation: increment only when a dialog is actually handled/blocked.

Issue 3: Performance: broad selectors and repeated candidate filter.
- lines 13-19 dialogSels include `[class*="popup"]`, `.modal`; lines 62-75 _pqQueryAll; lines 86-99 _pqIsDialogCandidate queries options/buttons for each candidate; lines 207-218 fallback scans all buttons.
- consequence: high CPU on large pages each tick.
- recommendation: narrow selectors, cache results within tick, reduce fallback frequency.

Issue 4: _pqPruneHandled depends on CONST.PQ_HANDLED_CAP without fallback.
- lines 41-49: `if (_ks.length > CONST.PQ_HANDLED_CAP)`. If CONST undefined, ReferenceError caught by bestEffort but prune skipped, memory leak.
- consequence: _pqHandled grows indefinitely.
- recommendation: use local cap or check CONST existence.

Issue 5: Dialog candidate misclassification risk.
- lines 80-99: class/regex checks; line 121 `_pqRemove` removes element.
- consequence: could delete important page content if element contains semantic button or class.
- recommendation: require stronger dialog markers or parent context.

Issue 6: _pqSiteGuard hardcoded allowed list.
- line 183: `_pqAllowedSites = ['zhihuishu', 'icourse163', 'xuetangx', 'icve']`.
- This is okay but maintainability issue. Could mention.

#### `quiz-selectors.js`

Issue 1: createQuizSelectors doesn't validate argument.
- line 4-11: `questionSels: ['.question-item', '.quiz-item', thirdQuestionSel, ...]`.
- consequence: passing null/undefined produces invalid selector 'undefined'.
- recommendation: validate or default to empty string.

Issue 2: Broad attribute selectors.
- lines 6-9: `[class*="question"]`, `[class*="quiz"]`, `[class*="choice"]`.
- consequence: may match unrelated elements; false positives.
- recommendation: site-calibrate and remove broad selectors after calibration.

# 演示/UI 层代码审查报告

## 1. commands.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\commands.js:154,172`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（XSS / innerHTML 注入）、可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 命令下拉列表通过字符串拼接后写入 `innerHTML`：
  ```js
  if (!list.length) { box.style.display = 'none'; box.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  // ...
  box.innerHTML = html; box.style.display = 'block';
  ```
  虽然对命令名、描述使用了 `escapeHTML`，但仍依赖 `innerHTML` 注入动态内容，且与项目倡导的 `h()` + `setSafeText` DOM 构建方式不一致。
- **潜在后果**: 一旦后续维护者遗漏转义，或 `escapeHTML` 被绕过，可导致命令面板 XSS；同时每次键盘输入都触发完整 DOM 重建，性能差。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `h()` 创建每条命令项，文本用 `textContent` 填充，完全移除 `innerHTML`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\commands.js:127`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**: `/ninja` 命令参数解析只判断是否等于 `off`：
  ```js
  var on = !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim());
  ```
  没有处理 `0`，也没有显式处理 `on`。
- **潜在后果**: `/ninja 0` 会被当成“开”，与 `/loop 0`、`/debug 0` 的语义不一致；任何非 `off` 参数都会开启 ninja，体验混乱。
- **改进建议**: 统一参数解析逻辑：`on`/`1` 开、`off`/`0` 关，否则切换。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\commands.js:114,132`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: `/mute`、`/single` 使用了与 `/ninja` 同样的松散解析：
  ```js
  var on = arg ? !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim()) : !fg.muted;
  var on = arg ? !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim()) : !CONFIG.SINGLE_VIDEO;
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 参数语义不一致，容易误触发。
- **改进建议**: 提取公共的 `parseOnOff(arg, current)` 辅助函数，统一处理。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\commands.js:12`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常 / 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 从 `localStorage` 读取收藏夹后未校验类型：
  ```js
  _cxFavorites = safeGet(function () { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CX_KEYS.CMD_FAV) || '[]'); }, []);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 若用户或其他脚本写入非数组内容（如字符串、对象），`_cxFavorites.indexOf` 仍可工作但收藏/取消收藏逻辑可能异常。
- **改进建议**: 解析后校验 `Array.isArray(_cxFavorites)` 且元素为字符串，否则重置为 `[]`。

## 2. dashboard.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\dashboard.js:316-318`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（XSS / innerHTML）、性能瓶颈
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 视频列表整表通过 `innerHTML` 重建：
  ```js
  if (wrap.__cxLastHTML !== html || (html && wrap.childElementCount === 0)) {
    wrap.__cxLastHTML = html;
    wrap.innerHTML = html;
  }
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 每次刷新都销毁并重建全部视频行节点，事件委托虽可保留但 DOM 开销大；`__cxLastHTML` 字符串 diff 本身也是 O(n)。若某处转义遗漏，会引入 XSS。
- **改进建议**: 改为按视频索引进行差异更新，或至少使用 `documentFragment` + `h()` 构建节点，用 `textContent` 设置文本。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\dashboard.js:44,73`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（XSS 隐患）/ 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: Hero 时间区域使用 `innerHTML` 写入子元素：
  ```js
  if (heroTime) heroTime.innerHTML = '0:00<small> / 0:00</small>';
  // ...
  if (heroTime) heroTime.innerHTML = tCur + '<small> / ' + tDur + '</small>';
  ```
- **潜在后果**: `tCur`/`tDur` 目前来自 `fmtTime`（数字格式化），相对安全，但模式上违反了“动态内容走 DOM”的约定。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `h('span', ...)` 创建时间文本与小字号后缀，通过 `replaceChildren` 替换。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\dashboard.js:241-243`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 性能瓶颈
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 命中率采样在每次面板渲染时累加：
  ```js
  if (targetEnabled) { _targetHitSamples++; if (TARGET.matchedAny) _targetHitHits++; }
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 样本数随面板刷新频率增长，而非随实际定向决策次数增长；百分比受刷新频率影响，不能真实反映命中情况。
- **改进建议**: 在主循环每次做定向判断时采样，或在 `TARGET.matchedAny` 变化时采样。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\dashboard.js:122`
- **问题分类**: 可读性 / 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 条件缺少括号，依赖运算符优先级：
  ```js
  if (!st || !st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0) {
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 当前逻辑正确，但后续修改极易引入优先级错误。
- **改进建议**: 加括号明确意图：`if (!st || (!st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0))`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\dashboard.js:170-176`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 直接访问 `FLAGS` 常量对象：
  ```js
  if (v[FLAGS.endedLock]) hasEndLock = true;
  if (v[FLAGS.userPaused]) hasUserPause = true;
  if (v[FLAGS.forcePaused]) hasForcePause = true;
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 若 `FLAGS` 未定义或拼写错误，会抛出 `ReferenceError`，导致徽章更新中断。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `typeof FLAGS !== 'undefined'` 守卫，或在模块顶部显式依赖。

## 3. diagnostics.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\diagnostics.js:140-145`

  ```js
  if (document.body) { document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(ta); }
  EventBus.emit('ui:toast', '已复制诊断信息');
  ```

- **潜在后果**: 非安全上下文且 `body` 尚未就绪时，用户看到“已复制”，实际剪贴板未写入。
- **改进建议**: 将成功 toast 放入 `if (document.body)` 分支内，else 分支提示“复制失败，请手动复制”。

- **潜在后果**: 用户无法从面板直接看到失败原因，必须去控制台。

- **问题分类**: 安全问题（XSS / document.write）、可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 黑匣子导出失败时打开新窗口并 `document.write`：
  ```js
  var w = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=700,height=500');
  if (w) { w.document.write('<pre>' + text.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</pre>'); }
  ```
- **潜在后果**: `document.write` 已废弃且部分转义不完整（未处理 `>`、`"`、`'`）；若日志内容被污染，存在绕过风险。弹窗拦截器还会导致 `w` 为 `null`。
- **改进建议**: 使用 DOM API：
  ```js
  var pre = w.document.createElement('pre');
  pre.textContent = text;
  w.document.body.appendChild(pre);
  ```

## 5. panel-core.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\panel-core.js:39`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 未加保护地访问 `window.top.document`：
  ```js
  var pd = (window.top && window.top.document && window.top.document.body) ? window.top.document : document;
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 在跨域 iframe 中读取 `window.top.document` 会抛出 `SecurityError`，导致 `ensurePanel` 中断、面板无法创建。
- **改进建议**: 用 try/catch 安全探测顶层文档：
  ```js
  var pd = document;
  try { if (window.top && window.top.document && window.top.document.body) pd = window.top.document; } catch (e) {}
  ```

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\panel-core.js:67`

- **具体描述与代码片段**: 面板骨架通过 `innerHTML` 注入：
  ```js
  el.innerHTML = buildPanelHTML(_inFrame);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 模板虽为内部生成，但 `innerHTML` 仍会绕过 CSP 限制；与项目 DOM-safe 策略不一致。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `h()` 与 `documentFragment` 逐块构建，或至少对模板中所有动态插值强制转义。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\panel-core.js:277-286`

- **具体描述与代码片段**: Ninja 标题栏 `mouseup` 监听未排除按钮子元素：
  ```js
  ninjaBar.addEventListener('mouseup', function (e) {
    if (!el.classList.contains('ninja')) return;
    if (el.classList.contains('cx-dragging')) return;
    // ...
    el.classList.toggle('ninja-open');
  });
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 点击“退出 n”按钮或关闭按钮时，若事件冒泡到标题栏，可能误触发 ninja-open 切换。
- **改进建议**: 在 handler 内检查 `e.target.closest('button, .cx-icon-btn, .cx-exit-ninja')`，若是则直接返回。

- **潜在后果**: 代码含义不清，误导维护者认为拖拽仍存在。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\panel-template.js:55-314`

- **具体描述与代码片段**: `buildPanelHTML` 返回数百行 HTML 字符串，最终通过 `panel-core.js` 的 `innerHTML` 注入。
- **潜在后果**: 大段字符串模板难以维护、无语法高亮与校验；任何插值遗漏转义都会成为 XSS 入口。
- **改进建议**: 拆分为可复用的 `h()` 组件，或至少使用 `<template>` 元素克隆；对 `SCRIPT_VERSION`、`_inFrame` 等插值保持转义。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\panel-template.js:312`
- **问题分类**: 可维护性缺陷

  ```js

  ```
- **潜在后果**: `SCRIPT_VERSION` 是内部常量，当前安全；但模式上不符合统一转义规范。
- **改进建议**: 统一调用 `escapeHTML` 处理所有动态插值。

## 9. panel-toast-feed.js

## 10. panel.js

## 12. toast.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\toast.js:39`
- **问题分类**: 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 模块加载时同步读取 localStorage：
  ```js
  bestEffort(function () { if (localStorage.getItem(CX_KEYS.QUIET_POPUPS) === '1') _quietPopups = true; }, 'toast.quietLoad');
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 虽被 `bestEffort` 捕获，但模块加载阶段访问 localStorage 仍可能因浏览器隐私模式抛出并被吞掉。

- **具体描述与代码片段**: 仅 `critical`/`error` 级别的 toast 才会悬浮：
  ```js
  var _FLOAT_LEVELS = { critical: 1, error: 1 };
  if (!_FLOAT_LEVELS[level]) return;
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 这是按设计实现，但意味着 `info`/`success`/`warn` 默认不会悬浮，初次使用可能误以为 toast 系统失效。
- **改进建议**: 在代码注释或面板提示中说明“普通提示仅进入洞察页提示流”。

## 13. zhihuishu-fab.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\zhihuishu-fab.js:282-289`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误（严重）
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 使用 `EventBus.on` 订阅事件：
  ```js
  if (typeof EventBus !== 'undefined' && EventBus.on) {
    EventBus.on('zhExam:diag', function (d) { ... });
  }
  ```
- **潜在后果**: `EventBus.on` 订阅的是状态键（key），而事件通过 `emit('zhExam:diag')` 发送到 `subs['__ev:zhExam:diag']`。因此该监听器永远不会被触发，`_zhsDiagReason` 无法更新，面板诊断提示失效。
- **改进建议**: 改为 `EventBus.onEv('zhExam:diag', function (d) { ... })`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\zhihuishu-fab.js:231-232,247,252`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（innerHTML）、可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 智慧树浮层内容通过 `innerHTML` 设置：
  ```js
  pop.innerHTML = (mode === 'exam') ? _zhsExamPopHTML() : _zhsVideoPopHTML();
  fab.innerHTML = ZHS_FAB_SVG + '<span class="cx-zhs-dot"></span>';
  pop.innerHTML = _zhsIsExamPage() ? _zhsExamPopHTML() : _zhsVideoPopHTML();
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 与面板模板同样存在维护性与 XSS 隐患。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `h()` 或 `documentFragment` 构建浮层。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\zhihuishu-fab.js:83-88`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（潜在 XSS）
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 头部 HTML 未转义 label 与 class：
  ```js
  return '<div class="cx-zhs-head">' +
    '<span class="cx-zhs-badge ' + (badgeClass || '') + '">' + label + '</span>' +
    ...
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 当前 `label` 与 `badgeClass` 为硬编码中文/常量，但若后续改为动态值，会直接注入 HTML。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `escapeHTML` 或改用 `h()` 构建。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\zhihuishu-fab.js:24`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 安全问题
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 题目页判定正则过于宽泛：
  ```js
  return /dohomework|webExamList|stuExamWeb|doExam|exam|homework|doHomeWork/i.test(...);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 任何 URL 包含 `exam` 或 `homework` 都会被识别为题目页，可能误触发。
- **改进建议**: 使用更精确的路径匹配，如 `/(^|\/)dohomework\//i` 等。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\zhihuishu-fab.js:157-158,163`
- **问题分类**: 性能瓶颈 / 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**: `_zhsRefreshExam` 中多次读取同一 localStorage 键：
  ```js
  var ans = document.getElementById('__cxZheAns'); if (ans) ans.checked = safeGet(function () { return localStorage.getItem(CX_KEYS.ZH_EXAM_ON); }, null) === '1';
  var sub = document.getElementById('__cxZheSub'); if (sub) sub.checked = safeGet(function () { return localStorage.getItem(CX_KEYS.ZH_EXAM_AUTO_SUBMIT); }, null) === '1';
  // ...
  var _ansOn = safeGet(function () { return localStorage.getItem(CX_KEYS.ZH_EXAM_ON); }, null) === '1';
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 同一刷新周期内重复读取 localStorage，增加同步 IO。
- **改进建议**: 在函数开头读取一次并缓存到局部变量。

## 14. chaoxing-force-play.user.js

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\chaoxing-force-play.user.js:7-19`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（过度授权注入范围）
- **具体描述与代码片段**: `@match` 规则过于宽泛：
  ```js
  // @match        *://*.chaoxing.com/*
  // @match        *://*.edu.cn/*
  // @match        *://*.zhihuishu.com/*
  // ...
  ```
- **潜在后果**: `*://*.edu.cn/*` 会注入到所有 `.edu.cn` 域名下的所有页面，包括非课程系统，存在隐私泄露与意外行为风险。
- **改进建议**: 缩小匹配范围，至少限定到具体课程平台路径（如 `*://*.chaoxing.com/mycourse/*`）。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\chaoxing-force-play.user.js:497`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（postMessage 目标源）
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 跨帧转发 toast 时使用通配目标源：
  ```js
  window.top.postMessage({ __cxFeedToast: Array.from(arguments).slice(1) }, '*');
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 任何可访问当前窗口的源都能接收到消息；虽然消息内容只是 toast 文本，但违反了最小权限原则。
- **改进建议**: 使用 `'/'` 或当前 `location.origin` 作为 `targetOrigin`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\chaoxing-force-play.user.js:336-340`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 安全问题（属性处理）
- **具体描述与代码片段**: `h()` 助手对非真实事件属性执行 `setAttribute`：
  ```js
  if (('on' + _evt) in el) el.addEventListener(_evt, v);
  else el.setAttribute(k, v);
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 若属性名以 `on` 开头但不是标准事件（如 `only` 不会命中，但 `ondata` 等可能误判），会把函数 `toString()` 写入属性；若该函数体含特殊内容，可能产生意外 HTML。
- **改进建议**: 维护一个标准事件白名单，仅对白名单内的事件名绑定监听器。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\chaoxing-force-play.user.js:7208`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（XSS / document.write）
- **具体描述与代码片段**: 黑匣子导出 fallback 使用 `document.write` 且仅转义 `&` 和 `<`：
  ```js
  if (w) { w.document.write('<pre>' + text.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</pre>'); }
  ```
- **潜在后果**: 同 `panel-controls.js` 的分析；`document.write` 已废弃，且部分转义不可靠。
- **改进建议**: 与面板代码一致，使用 `w.document.createElement('pre').textContent = text`。

## 汇总

| 文件 | 主要问题 |
|------|----------|
| `commands.js` | `innerHTML` 动态生成命令列表；参数解析（`off`/`0`）不一致 |
| `dashboard.js` | `innerHTML` 整表重建视频列表；Hero 区域 `innerHTML`；命中率采样与渲染频率耦合 |
| `diagnostics.js` | `document.body` 缺失时仍提示复制成功 |
| `panel-controls.js` | `document.write` 导出黑匣子；JSON.parse 位置不当；`_cdn.checked` 未触发事件 |
| `panel-core.js` | 跨域 `window.top.document` 会抛异常；面板骨架 `innerHTML`；Ninja 标题栏点击冲突 |
| `panel-drag.js` | 仅含注释，为死代码文件 |
| `panel-ds-console.js` | 应答端状态硬编码；ticker 面板关闭后仍在跑 |
| `panel-template.js` | 巨型 HTML 字符串模板；插值转义不统一 |
| `panel-toast-feed.js` | 跨窗口 DOM 访问依赖外层 safeGet |
| `panel.js` | `syncNinjaGlyph` 未使用 `allVideos()`；全局键盘监听无法卸载 |
| `styles.js` | 巨型 CSS 字符串；空 `NINJA_STYLE` 冗余 |
| `toast.js` | 模块加载时同步读 localStorage |
| `zhihuishu-fab.js` | **`EventBus.on` 误用导致诊断事件丢失**；`innerHTML`；正则过宽 |
| `chaoxing-force-play.user.js` | `@match` 过宽；`postMessage('*')`；`h()` 事件判定不严格 |

**最高优先级修复**：
1. `zhihuishu-fab.js:282` 将 `EventBus.on` 改为 `EventBus.onEv`。
2. `panel-core.js:39` 安全处理跨域 `window.top.document`。
3. 逐步将 `innerHTML` 替换为 `h()` + `textContent`（命令列表、视频列表、面板骨架）。

Read package.json maybe has build order.

Build script is powershell. We can read it to know order.

Build order revealed. Important: storage.js is loaded AFTER config.js (line 66 vs 64). Wait build order: keys, utils, url, guard, state, metrics, config, styles, storage, site-router, ... That means config.js calls `loadPanelCfg()` and `loadWatchStats()` at top level BEFORE storage.js defines them! Since function declarations hoist, `loadPanelCfg` and `loadWatchStats` are declared in storage.js but execution reaches config.js before storage.js. However function declarations are hoisted to top of IIFE, so at the moment config.js runs, `loadPanelCfg` is already defined. So okay. Similarly `EventBus` is defined in state.js before config (state before config). Good. So load order is safe. But there is a subtle issue: `loadPanelCfg` references `CONFIG` and `DEBUG` (defined in config.js). Since config.js calls loadPanelCfg at top level, and loadPanelCfg runs after CONFIG/DEBUG declared in same file (top-level execution order within config.js). So fine.

But `storage.js` defines `KVS` and uses `CONFIG` in backendName. At time storage.js runs, CONFIG already defined. Good.

Note: `config.js` loaded before `site-router.js`. `usePrototypeNeutralize` calls `detectSite` which is defined in site-router later, but function declarations hoist. However it calls `detectSite()` at line 130 and 168 at top level (immediately when config.js executes). Since `detectSite` is a function declaration in site-router.js, hoisted to top of IIFE, so defined before config runs. Good.

`config.js` also calls `loadPanelCfg()` and `loadWatchStats()` top-level. Those functions use `localStorage`, safe.

Thus no load order runtime errors.

Now check build order for `quiz.js` dependencies: popup-quiz.js is loaded before quiz.js. So `_pqQueryAll` defined. Good. quiz-merge before quiz. Good. quiz-vision before quiz? Actually quiz-vision after quiz in build order. But quiz.js references `quizRecover` function defined in quiz-vision.js. Since function declaration hoisted, it's defined before quiz.js runs? Wait build order: quiz.js at line 88, quiz-vision.js at line 89. So quiz.js is loaded before quiz-vision.js. But quiz.js references `quizRecover` only inside `_quizVisionAnswer` at runtime. Function declarations from quiz-vision.js are hoisted to top of IIFE, so by the time `_quizVisionAnswer` is called (after all files loaded), `quizRecover` exists. Good. Similarly `quizAskDeepSeek` in quiz-vision called by quiz-vision; vision-deepseek loaded after quiz-vision, function hoisted. Good.

Similarly `mergeAnswers`/`decisionFor` from quiz-merge loaded before quiz. Good.

Now `vision-deepseek-web.js` loaded after quiz-vision. It defines `quizAskDeepSeek`. `quiz-vision.js` references `quizAskDeepSeek` at runtime (inside quizRecover). Hoisted. Good.

Now `dom/session.js` loaded before `lifecycle.js`? Build order: dom.js, session.js, toast, lifecycle.js. `lifecycle.js` references `allVideos`, `EventBus`, etc. Function hoisted. Good.

Now `main-loop.js` references `playWatchHandler` defined in itself, `_loopTimer` var. Good.

Now `_cxPanel` etc referenced in main-loop and lifecycle defined in presentation modules after dom/lifecycle in build order. But references are at runtime inside functions, and presentation modules run before main-loop? Actually main-loop is last. So functions called after presentation loaded. Good.

Now `globalErrorHandler` defined in core.js, referenced in lifecycle.js. Core first. Good.

Now `_playWatchDocs` declared in lifecycle.js but written in main-loop.js. lifecycle loaded after main-loop? Build order: dom.js, session.js, toast.js, lifecycle.js, ... main-loop last. So `_playWatchDocs` declared in lifecycle.js before main-loop.js runs. Good.

Now `_ananasNeutralized` declared in lifecycle.js, written in dom.js. dom.js loaded before lifecycle? Build order: dom.js then session, toast, lifecycle. So when dom.js runs, `_ananasNeutralized` not yet declared? But `var` declarations hoist across IIFE, so it's declared. However assignment `var _ananasNeutralized = []` in lifecycle.js occurs after dom.js top-level code? But dom.js only writes to `_ananasNeutralized` inside function `neutralizeGlobalPause` at runtime, after lifecycle loaded. So fine.

Similarly `_origMediaSessionPause`, `_mediaSessionSaved` declared in lifecycle.js but written in dom.js `_ovEnforce`. dom.js loaded before lifecycle, but write occurs later. Fine.

Now `keydownHandler`, `panelNinjaOutsideClick`, `syncNinjaGlyph` referenced in lifecycle but defined in presentation modules loaded after lifecycle? Build order: lifecycle before panel.js etc. But lifecycle only references them inside `cleanupListeners` function, called at runtime after all modules loaded. Function declarations hoisted. Good.

Now `toast` referenced in utils.js toolkit (line 251 `else toast(m, lv || 'info');`) but `toast` defined in presentation/toast.js loaded after utils. Function declaration hoisted. Good.

Now `SITE_TICKS` etc.

Now identify issues with confidence.

Let's do a more detailed pass per file and produce findings.

#### meta.js
No executable code. The `@version` says 4.18, package.json 4.15.0. Not an issue? Inconsistency maybe. Also @match includes `https://chat.deepseek.com/*`. Good.

#### foundation/utils/utils.js
Issues:
1. `fetchWithTimeout` synchronous fetch exception leaks timer (line 121-127). If `fetch(url, init)` throws synchronously, `id` is not cleared and AbortController timer will fire, calling `ac.abort()` after function thrown. Potential unhandled rejection? Actually `ac.abort()` after fetch thrown may not cause rejection; but timer leak. Also no catch for synchronous throw. Runtime exception category. Also timer not cleared if fetch throws synchronously. Potential consequence: leaked timer + uncaught exception aborts caller. Improve with try/catch around fetch to clearTimeout and rethrow.
2. `h` function appends `typeof c === 'string' || typeof c === 'number'` else as node. If `c` is boolean or object not Node, `appendChild` will throw or append object (e.g. Array? Actually if children is array of arrays, first branch? It wraps children in array then loops; if c is array, it goes to else and appendChild(array) -> TypeError. Potential runtime. But callers may not pass arrays of arrays. However signature says children can be Array; if nested arrays, issue. Could be considered robustness. Maybe report.
3. `h` function line 216 `if (('on' + _evt) in el) el.addEventListener(_evt, v); else el.setAttribute(k, v);`. For custom event names not in element's `on*` properties (e.g. `oncustom`), it sets attribute to function, which stringifies to source or `[object Function]` causing potential XSS or weird attribute. Should always use addEventListener for event-like keys and avoid setAttribute with function. Security/maintainability.
4. `lsFlag` / `lsSetFlag` use raw keys while lsGetJSON uses prefixed. Could be intentional, but potential naming collision? Not major.

But `window.top === window.self` throws for cross-origin? Actually comparing `window.top === window.self` does not throw cross-origin? Accessing `window.top` is allowed; comparing window references is allowed. The try/catch is for `window.top` access? It's safe. Why catch? Not an issue.

#### foundation/storage/storage.js
1. `KVS.set` / `del` / `get` for idb backend do not handle transaction `onerror` / `oncomplete`. `set` creates transaction and put but doesn't wait or catch async errors. If put fails, data may not persist and no fallback after async error. Could lead to silent data loss. The `try/catch` only catches synchronous exceptions. Async IDB errors not handled. Runtime/robustness.
2. `KVS.get` resolves `rq.result == null ? null : String(rq.result)`. If result is object, String(object) -> '[object Object]'. But set stores String, so okay.

4. `savePanelCfg` only saves subset; e.g. `CONFIG.KVS_BACKEND` not persisted. Fine.
5. `loadPanelCfg` uses `localStorage.getItem` without `safeGet`; localStorage access can throw (private mode). It wraps whole in try/catch. Good.

#### foundation/state/state.js
1. `emit` line 34: postMessage to `window.top` with targetOrigin '*'. This forwards toast messages to parent frame. If running on a page with a hostile parent, leaks toast content (could contain internal status/diagnostics). Security: should use `window.location.origin` as targetOrigin to restrict. Also if `window.top` is cross-origin, postMessage with '*' still works but less restrictive. This is a concrete security issue.
2. `clearSubs`: line 52 `subs = {};` reassigns variable inside closure. If called while iterating? Not.

4. The state mirror note: EventBus.state only mirrors references; not an issue.

Let's double-check `_ovForegroundGate` logic to ensure our interpretation correct. Code:
```
function _ovForegroundGate(v, fg) {
    if (!fg || v === fg) return false;   // 无前台或本视频即前台：不接管，交主流程
    if (!CONFIG.SINGLE_VIDEO && cxState(v).userKeep) return false;   // 非单视频模式：用户显式保留的视频不干预
    // 单视频模式：忽略 userKeep，强制暂停所有非前台视频
    if (v[FLAGS.forcePaused]) {
      bestEffort(function () { releaseVideo(v); }, 'ov.fg.release');
      bestEffort(function () { if (!v.paused) (v[FLAGS.np] || NATIVE_PAUSE).call(v); }, 'ov.fg.pause');
    }
    return true;   // 非前台视频已强制暂停，由本 gate 接管；true=跳过主 override 管线
  }
```
Yes, if SINGLE_VIDEO true and v not fg and not forcePaused, it returns true without pausing. So background video not paused. It will be skipped from enforce (because return true). So it stays as is. If it was playing, continues playing. Thus SINGLE_VIDEO fails. Also if not SINGLE_VIDEO and not userKeep, returns false -> enforce continues -> may play it. Wait comment says non-single mode userKeep preserved, but other videos? It returns false to let main flow handle. Main flow `_ovEnforce` will safePlay if paused. So non-userKeep background videos will also be played. That seems contradictory to "前台闸门". But maybe in non-single-video mode, the intention is to allow multiple videos and only foreground gate handles userKeep? Hmm. This is likely a design bug. The function's name suggests foreground gate should enforce only foreground video gets played. But the code doesn't pause non-foreground in non-single mode. It only prevents enforce for userKeep? Actually userKeep returns false, meaning enforce continues, which would not pause it either. So userKeep is not preserved; it is still played if paused. Wait if userKeep true and not single, return false -> enforce -> safePlay if paused. That means userKeep doesn't keep paused. Contradiction. Maybe userKeep means "keep playing"? In `userResume`, userKeep set true for non-foreground videos to prevent gate from pausing them in single mode. In non-single mode, userKeep is irrelevant. So the comment "用户显式保留的视频不干预" maybe means don't pause them in single mode. But code returns false, leading to enforce. However in single mode, the userKeep branch is after `!CONFIG.SINGLE_VIDEO`, so ignored. So in single mode, userKeep videos also get paused? Actually line 45 only runs if `!CONFIG.SINGLE_VIDEO`. In single mode, it skips to line 47. So userKeep not considered. That contradicts "单视频模式：忽略 userKeep". The code does ignore userKeep in single mode (good), but doesn't pause non-forcePaused videos. So bug is clear.

Maybe the intended logic: In single mode, for any non-foreground video that is currently forcePaused (i.e., script has taken over), release and pause it. For non-foreground not yet taken over, return true to skip enforce (don't take over). But if it's playing natively, it remains playing. That's not "强制暂停". Unless platform natively pauses background videos and script only needs to release its own. But the comment says强制暂停所有非前台视频.

Maybe there is a missing else: it should always release+pause in single mode. So yes logic bug.

Another issue: In non-single mode, it returns false for non-foreground non-userKeep, allowing enforce. Maybe intended? But then multiple videos play. Hmm.

Should we report both? We can report the single-video bug concretely; the non-single behavior might be intentional but inconsistent. Maybe mention as logic/design issue: foreground gate does not actually pause background videos in non-single mode; it only skips enforcing userKeep? It's confusing. But to avoid over-reporting, focus on single-video bug.

### 1. `chaoxing-exam.js`

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\chaoxing-exam.js:26`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常 / 依赖顺序
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  registerSiteTick('chaoxing', chaoxingTickQuiz);
  ```
  文件加载时直接调用 `registerSiteTick`。若 `registerSiteTick` 所在核心脚本尚未初始化，会抛出 `ReferenceError`，整个模块注册失败。
- **潜在后果**: 超星作业/考试页的真答题模块未注册，面板与自动作答不可用。
- **改进建议**: 将注册逻辑包装在 `bestEffort` 中，或等待 `window.__cxRegisterSiteTick` 等核心入口就绪后再注册。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\chaoxing-exam.js:13-18`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误（选择器过宽）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  questionSels: ['.Zy_ulTop', '.questionLi', '.questionBox', '.topic-item', '.qItem', '.question-item', '.examPaper',
                 '[class*="question"]', '[class*="topic"]', '[class*="ques"]', '.questionnaire'],
  ```
  包含 `[class*="question"]`、`[class*="topic"]` 等过宽属性选择器，容易命中页面上的目录、导航、讨论区等非题目容器。
- **潜在后果**: quizTick 可能把普通容器当题目处理，造成误判、乱点或提交异常。
- **改进建议**: 在真实 DOM 校准后移除兜底属性选择器，或增加更强的题目特征过滤。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\chaoxing-exam.js:15-17`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误（题干选择器缺失）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  stemSels: ['.questionTitle', '.qItemTitle', '.topic-title', '.stem', '[class*="title"]', '[class*="stem"]'],
  optionSels: ['.Zy_ulTop li.font-cxsecret', ...]
  ```
  注释已说明“题干不在 `.Zy_ulTop` 内”，但 `stemSels` 未覆盖超星作业页真实题干容器，bank/AI 源无法精确匹配题干。
- **潜在后果**: 题库/AI 答题准确率下降，只能依赖随机源兜底。
- **改进建议**: 根据真实 `doHomeWorkNew` 页面补充题干容器选择器。

  ```js
  function _pqHandle(dialog) {
    if (!dialog) return false;
    var fp = _pqFingerprint(dialog);
    if (_pqHandled[fp]) return false;
    _pqHandled[fp] = true; _pqPruneHandled();
    bestEffort(function () {
      _pqPickRandom(dialog);
      _pqClickAnswer(dialog);
      _pqDismiss(dialog);
      _pqPopups++; _pqSnapshot();
      ...

    return true;
  }
  ```
  在实际点击、提交、移除弹窗前就先把指纹标记为已处理。
- **潜在后果**: 若 `click()` 未真正触发关闭（事件被拦截、选择器未命中可点击元素等），该弹窗后续不会再被处理，页面卡死。

- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误（风暴计数口径）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function _pqCheckStorm() {
    var now = Date.now();
    if (now - _pqStormGate.lastReset > 10000) { ... }
    ...
    _pqStormGate.count++;
    if (_pqStormGate.count > _pqStormGate.maxPer10sec) { ... }
    return false;
  }
  ```
  每次 `popupQuizTick` / `popupQuizBlock` 被调用都会计数，而不是“实际关闭弹窗”才计数。
- **潜在后果**: 即使没有弹窗，只要主循环调用频率高，就会触发 30 秒静默，导致真实弹窗无人处理。
- **改进建议**: 仅在成功处理/关闭一个弹窗时才递增计数。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\popup-quiz.js:62-99`
- **问题分类**: 性能瓶颈
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function _pqQueryAll(root, sels) {
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < sels.length; i++) {
      var ns = root.querySelectorAll(sels[i]);
      for (var j = 0; j < ns.length; j++) out.push(ns[j]);
    }
    return out;
  }
  function _pqIsDialogCandidate(el) {
    ...
    var opts = safeGet(function () { return el.querySelectorAll(POPUP_QUIZ.optionSels.join(',')); }, null);
    var btns = safeGet(function () { return el.querySelectorAll('button, a[class*="btn"]'); }, null);
    ...
  }
  ```
  `dialogSels` 包含 `[class*="popup"]`、`.modal` 等极宽选择器，每次 tick 都会对所有命中元素再做两次 `querySelectorAll` 校验。
- **潜在后果**: 在大型单页应用上每次 tick 都可能扫描数千个节点，导致主线程卡顿。
- **改进建议**: 收紧弹窗选择器；对 `_pqQueryAll` 结果去重；在单 tick 内缓存 `getBoundingClientRect` 与选项查询结果。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\popup-quiz.js:41-49`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常 / 内存泄漏
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function _pqPruneHandled() {
    bestEffort(function () {
      var _ks = Object.keys(_pqHandled);
      if (_ks.length > CONST.PQ_HANDLED_CAP) {
        var _drop = _ks.length - CONST.PQ_HANDLED_CAP;
        for (var _p = 0; _p < _drop; _p++) delete _pqHandled[_ks[_p]];
      }
    }, 'pq.pruneHandled');
  }
  ```
  依赖全局 `CONST.PQ_HANDLED_CAP`。若 `CONST` 未定义，`bestEffort` 会吞掉 `ReferenceError`，但去重表将不再裁剪。
- **潜在后果**: 长时间运行会话中 `_pqHandled` 无限增长，内存占用持续上升。
- **改进建议**: 使用本地常量（如 `1000`）作为 fallback，或在访问前判断 `typeof CONST !== 'undefined'`。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\popup-quiz.js:80-125`

  ```js
    return false;

  function _pqRemove(dialog) {
    if (!safeGet(function () { ... dialog.parentNode.removeChild(dialog) ... }, false)) {
      bestEffort(function () { dialog.style.display = 'none'; ... }, 'pq.remove.hide');
    }
  }
  ```
  候选判定仅靠类名/按钮文本；若页面中某个重要面板（如安全提示、考试须知）包含“确定”按钮或 `dialog` 类名，可能被判定为弹窗并移除。
- **潜在后果**: 误删页面关键元素，甚至隐藏防作弊/安全提示。
- **改进建议**: 增加父级上下文校验（如必须在顶层 body 下、z-index 较高、有遮罩共存等），且删除前进行二次确认。

- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 启动时序
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  if (!(window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY && typeof window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.detectSite === 'function' && window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.detectSite() === 'zhihuishu')) return;
  ```
  若本模块先于核心脚本执行，`__CX_FORCE_PLAY` 不存在，IIFE 直接退出且不会再执行。
- **潜在后果**: 智慧树考试/作业页自动作答功能完全缺失。
- **改进建议**: 延迟启动或轮询等待 `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY` 就绪，而不是在解析期直接 return。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\zhihuishu-exam.js:96-102`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误（选项勾选可能失效）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function _zheSelect(optEl) {
    bestEffort(function () { optEl.click(); }, 'zhex.select.click');
    bestEffort(function () {
      var inp = optEl.querySelector ? optEl.querySelector('input[type="radio"],input[type="checkbox"]') : null;
      if (inp) { inp.checked = true; bestEffort(function () { inp.click(); }, 'zhex.select.inpClick'); }
    }, 'zhex.select.inp');
  }
  ```
  当 `optEl` 是 `<label>` 包裹 `<input>` 时，第一次 `optEl.click()` 已会触发 input 切换；随后设置 `inp.checked = true` 再 `inp.click()` 可能把 checkbox 再次切换为未选中。
- **潜在后果**: 选项实际未被选中，`_allAnsweredHere` 检测不到已答，导致反复尝试或无法进入 READY 状态。
- **改进建议**: 不要对同一选项同时 click label 与 click input；选中后通过 `dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }))` 触发业务校验。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\zhihuishu-exam.js:386-389`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常 / 原生 API 篡改
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  history.pushState = function () { var r = _op.apply(this, arguments); onRoute(); return r; };
  history.replaceState = function () { var r = _or.apply(this, arguments); onRoute(); return r; };
  ```
  重写 `history.pushState/replaceState` 后，`onRoute` 内部调用 `reSync()` 未再包裹 `bestEffort`。若 `sync()` 中 localStorage/EventBus 抛异常，会冒泡到页面自身的路由调用方。
- **潜在后果**: 破坏智慧树 SPA 正常翻页/跳转，控制台出现未捕获异常。
- **改进建议**: 在 `onRoute` 内部用 `bestEffort(reSync, 'zhex.route')` 包裹，确保异常不影响页面原生路由。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\zhihuishu-exam.js:210、253、261`
- **问题分类**: 可维护性缺陷（死代码）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  var _diagDS = true, _diagBlocked = '', _diagLastTs = 0, _DIAG_MIN_GAP = 8000;
  ...
  _diagDS = true;
  ...
  _diagDS = _dsOk();
  ```
  `_diagDS` 被赋值但从未读取。
- **潜在后果**: 增加维护者困惑，无实际功能影响。
- **改进建议**: 移除 `_diagDS` 或将其用于诊断状态输出。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\sites\zhihuishu-exam.js:40-41`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误（选择器过宽）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  nextSels: ['.next-page', '.next', '.next-btn', '[class*="next"]', '[class*="page-next"]', 'a[title*="下一"]', 'a[title*="下一项"]'],
  submitSels: ['.submit-btn', '.answer-btn', 'button[class*="submit"]', '.confirm-btn', '.tk-submit', '.btn-submit']
  ```
  `[class*="next"]` 会命中大量无关元素（如“下一步”、“下一个任务”）。
- **潜在后果**: 自动翻页/交卷可能点错按钮。

- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js

    ...

    ...
  } else {

  }
  ```
  只处理了绝对路径、根相对路径、`#` 锚点与 `javascript:`；对于相对查询字符串如 `?knowledgeId=123`（Bridge 与部分 DOM 链接常见）会直接报“无法解析”。
- **潜在后果**: Bridge 返回的下一节地址或 DOM 中的 `href="?knowledgeId=..."` 无法跳转，自动下一课失效。
- **改进建议**: 增加 `url.charAt(0) === '?'` 分支，使用 `location.search = url` 或拼接 `location.href` 导航。

  一旦探测所有 Bridge 端口失败，`allDead` 将保持为 `true` 直到页面刷新。

  旧环境使用 `v.src` 作为键；若多个视频 `src` 相同或为空，会去重错误。

### 9. `keyboard-shortcuts.js`

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\addons\keyboard-shortcuts.js:42-51`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 可访问性
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  if (e.code === PLAY_TOGGLE) {
    if (inText) return;
    if (!v) return;
    ...
    e.preventDefault();
    if (v.paused) v.play(); else v.pause();
  }
  ```
  只要不在文本输入框内，按下 Space 就会拦截默认行为并接管视频播放。
- **潜在后果**: 当焦点在按钮、链接、单选框等可聚焦元素上时，Space 的默认激活/选中行为被阻断，影响页面可访问性。
- **改进建议**: 增加 `e.target.tagName`/`role` 判断，仅在视频容器或页面空白处接管 Space。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\addons\keyboard-shortcuts.js:17-29`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  if (!v.offsetParent && v.offsetWidth === 0 && v.offsetHeight === 0) continue;
  ```
  该条件仅在 `offsetParent` 为 null 且宽高同时为 0 时才跳过，未判断 `display:none`、`visibility:hidden`。
- **潜在后果**: 可能把隐藏的视频当成“面积最大”的活跃视频。
- **改进建议**: 增加 `getComputedStyle` 对 `display`/`visibility`/`opacity` 的检查。

### 10. `quiet-popups.js`

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\addons\quiet-popups.js:15-28`
- **问题分类**: 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function isOn() {
    try {
      if (typeof window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY !== 'undefined' && typeof window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.isQuietPopups === 'function') return window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.isQuietPopups();
      return lsFlag(STORAGE_KEY, false);
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  ```
  逻辑合理，但没有站点隔离注释中也明确不需要；唯一问题是 storage 变更后无事件同步通知其他模块。
- **潜在后果**: 同一标签页内通过面板切换静默开关时，依赖 `localStorage` 事件，但该事件不会在当前标签页触发，可能导致同一页内其他模块状态不一致。
- **改进建议**: 切换时主动调用 `EventBus.emit('quiet-popups:change', v)` 等事件供各模块同步。

### 11. `tamper-guard.js`

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\addons\tamper-guard.js:67-69、98-103`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误 / 用户体验
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function notify() {
    ...
    else if (Notification.permission !== 'denied') Notification.requestPermission();
  }
  ...
  if (now - lastAlert > 3000) {
    ...
    tgToast(...);
    notify();
  }
  ```
  `lastAlert` 只节流 `tgToast`，但 `notify()` 每次都会调用 `Notification.requestPermission()`。
- **潜在后果**: 当接管被持续绕过时，每隔 3 秒重复请求通知权限，造成打扰。
- **改进建议**: 在 `notify()` 内部也做请求频率限制，或仅在用户首次开启功能时请求一次权限。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\addons\tamper-guard.js:106-113`
- **问题分类**: 逻辑错误
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  if (ns.CONFIG && ns.CONFIG.POLITE_MODE) {
    var vs = document.querySelectorAll('video');
    for (var i = 0; i < vs.length; i++) { ... if (!vv[TG_MUTED]) { ... vv.muted = true; } }
  }
  ```
  礼貌模式会对所有视频静音，但未排除已经暂停或不可见的视频。
- **潜在后果**: 后台/不可见视频被不必要静音，恢复时状态可能混乱。
- **改进建议**: 仅处理当前正在播放且可见的视频。

### 12. `video-ended-notify.js`


- **改进建议**: 改为 `(window.__cxAddonQueue = window.__cxAddonQueue || []).push(addon);`。

- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js

  ```
  注意这里虽然传了 `true`，但 `play` 事件**不冒泡**，document 捕获也捕获不到后代 `<video>` 的 `play`（媒体事件不沿 DOM 冒泡/捕获传播）。
- **潜在后果**: 视频播放时不会触发 `restoreIfNeeded`，被 `muteAll()` 静音的视频可能一直保持静音。
- **改进建议**: 改用 `document.addEventListener('playing', ..., true)`（`playing` 同样不冒泡）或在 `onThisPageEnded` 中直接监听每个 `video` 的 `play`/`playing` 事件。

  ```js

  ```
  视频结束时把当前页面 URL 与标题发送到本地 Bridge。
- **潜在后果**: 用户浏览的页面信息泄露到本地服务端口（虽然目的是多端联动，但无显式授权与脱敏）。
- **改进建议**: 在功能说明中明确告知数据上传范围，并提供关闭“多端联动”仅保留本地通知的开关。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\addons\video-ended-notify.js:163-168`

  ```js
  try { document.addEventListener('play', ..., true); } catch (e) {}
  ```
  未将事件监听器注册到 `window.__cxAddonCleanups`。
- **潜在后果**: 脚本卸载/禁用后，ended/play 监听仍然残留。

## 三、注册中心（registry.js）

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\registry.js:16-95`
- **问题分类**: 性能瓶颈
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  function renderAddons() {
    if (!_cxPanel) return;
    ...
    if (box) box.innerHTML = '';
    if (subBox) subBox.innerHTML = '';
    ids.forEach(function (id) { ... });
  }
  function drainAddonQueue() {
    ...
    renderAddons();
  }
  ```
  每次有 addon 注册都清空并重绘整个工具库区域。
- **潜在后果**: 多个 addon 顺序加载时产生多次 DOM 重绘。
- **改进建议**: 对 `drainAddonQueue`/`renderAddons` 做节流或防抖。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\registry.js:31-55`
- **问题分类**: 安全问题（subpanel 渲染不可信输入）
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  if (a.type === 'subpanel') {
    ...
    bestEffort(function () { a.render && a.render(bodyEl); }, 'reg.render');
  }
  ```
  调用 addon 提供的 `render` 函数并传入容器，脚本对渲染内容无限制。
- **潜在后果**: 若 addon 来源不可控，可能注入任意 HTML/执行任意 DOM 操作。
- **改进建议**: 在文档中明确 addon 为受信任本地代码；若允许第三方 addon，需对渲染内容进行沙箱/白名单审查。

- **文件路径与行号**: `c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\plugins\registry.js:17-22`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常 / 依赖缺失
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  ```js
  var box = _cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxAddons');
  ...
  block.style.cssText = STYLES.DIAG_BLOCK;
  ```
  依赖全局 `_cxPanel` 与 `STYLES`。虽然整体在 `bestEffort` 内，但任意一次异常会导致后续 addon 无法渲染。
- **潜在后果**: 面板样式或结构缺失时，工具库项不显示。
- **改进建议**: 对 `_cxPanel` 和 `STYLES` 做显式存在性校验，缺失时跳过渲染并记录诊断。

## 四、共性问题（多个文件）

- **涉及文件**: `sites/chaoxing-exam.js`、`icourse163.js`、`icve.js`、`xuetangx.js`、`ilabx.js`、`renwei.js`、`ucampus.js`、`unipus.js`、`zhihuishu.js`
- **问题分类**: 潜在运行时异常 / 依赖顺序
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  所有站点模块都在文件顶层直接调用 `registerSiteTick('xxx', fn)`。
- **潜在后果**: 核心脚本加载顺序一旦靠后，站点 tick 注册失败，对应平台功能全部失效。
- **改进建议**: 核心脚本应暴露 `__cxRegisterSiteTick`，站点模块在 `bestEffort` 中注册；或站点模块使用 `setTimeout`/轮询等待核心就绪。

- **涉及文件**: 所有 `sites/*.js` 与 `plugins/addons/*.js`
- **问题分类**: 可维护性缺陷
- **具体描述与代码片段**:
  大量选择器、关键词正则、localStorage key 以硬编码字面量分散在各文件中。
- **潜在后果**: 后续站点改版或 key 调整时，需要逐文件修改，容易遗漏。
- **改进建议**: 将选择器与键名进一步集中到共享配置文件（如 `sites/config.js` 或 `plugins/addons/config.js`），站点文件只做 thin adapter。
